import sys
import os
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import logging
sys.path.append(`
    r"C:\Users\hdagne1\Box\Dr.Mesfin Research\Codes\HortonIndex_Bidirectionality"`
)
from Horton_Index_bidirectionality.scripts.libraries import *
# --------------------------------------------------
# LOGGING
# --------------------------------------------------
logging.basicConfig(`
    level=logging.INFO,`
    format="%(levelname)s: %(message)s",`
    force=True`
)
# --------------------------------------------------
# PATHS
# --------------------------------------------------
BASE_DIR = (`
    r"C:\Users\hdagne1\Box\Dr.Mesfin Research\Codes"`
    r"\HortonIndex_Bidirectionality\Horton_Index_bidirectionality"
    r"\data\processed_data"
)
ET_PATH  = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "GLEAM_ET_total_Monthly.feather")
P_PATH   = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "P_6830_basins_monthly.feather")
PET_PATH = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "PET_6830_basins_monthly.feather")
Q_PATH   = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "Q_6830_basins_monthly.feather")
PERCENT_ERROR_THRESHOLD = 0.10
# --------------------------------------------------
# QUALITY CONTROL CLASS  (UNCHANGED LOGIC)
# --------------------------------------------------
class QualityControl6830:`
    @staticmethod
    def quality_control(ET_df, P_df, PET_df, Q_df, percent_error_threshold):
        common_gauges = (`
            ET_df.columns`
            .intersection(P_df.columns)
            .intersection(PET_df.columns)
            .intersection(Q_df.columns)
        )
        ET  = ET_df[common_gauges]
        P   = P_df[common_gauges]
        PET = PET_df[common_gauges]
        Q   = Q_df[common_gauges]
        common_time = (`
            ET.index`
            .intersection(P.index)
            .intersection(PET.index)
            .intersection(Q.index)
        )
        ET  = ET.loc[common_time]
        P   = P.loc[common_time]
        PET = PET.loc[common_time]
        Q   = Q.loc[common_time]
        ET_mean = ET.mean()
        P_mean  = P.mean()
        Q_mean  = Q.mean()
        ET_obs = P_mean - Q_mean
        ET_obs.replace(0, np.nan, inplace=True)
        percent_error = np.abs(ET_obs - ET_mean) / ET_obs
        valid_gauges = percent_error[
            percent_error <= percent_error_threshold
        ].dropna().index
        if valid_gauges.empty:
            raise ValueError("All basins failed QC.")
        filtered = {`
            "ET":  ET[valid_gauges],`
            "P":   P[valid_gauges],`
            "PET": PET[valid_gauges],`
            "Q":   Q[valid_gauges],`
        }
        logging.info("QC threshold        : %.2f", percent_error_threshold)
        logging.info("Basins before QC    : %d", len(common_gauges))
        logging.info("Basins after QC     : %d", len(valid_gauges))
        logging.info("Basins removed      : %d", len(common_gauges) - len(valid_gauges))
        return filtered, percent_error, valid_gauges
# --------------------------------------------------
# MAIN
# --------------------------------------------------
if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("Loading input data...")
    ET_df  = pd.read_feather(ET_PATH)
    P_df   = pd.read_feather(P_PATH)
    PET_df = pd.read_feather(PET_PATH)
    Q_df   = pd.read_feather(Q_PATH)
    #  CRITICAL FIX (YOU ALREADY VERIFIED THIS)
    ET_df  = ET_df.set_index("gauge_id").T
    P_df   = P_df.set_index("gauge_id").T
    PET_df = PET_df.set_index("gauge_id").T
    Q_df   = Q_df.set_index("gauge_id").T
    #  ENSURE DATETIME INDEX
    ET_df.index  = pd.to_datetime(ET_df.index)
    P_df.index   = pd.to_datetime(P_df.index)
    PET_df.index = pd.to_datetime(PET_df.index)
    Q_df.index   = pd.to_datetime(Q_df.index)
    print("Running quality control...")
    qc_data, percent_error, valid_gauges = QualityControl6830.quality_control(`
        ET_df=ET_df,`
        P_df=P_df,`
        PET_df=PET_df,`
        Q_df=Q_df,`
        percent_error_threshold=PERCENT_ERROR_THRESHOLD`
    )
    print("\n=========== QC SUMMARY ===========")
    print(f"Valid basins retained : {len(valid_gauges)}")
    print(f"Total basins checked  : {len(percent_error)}")
    print("\nPercent error statistics:")
    print(percent_error.describe())
    print("\nExample retained gauge IDs:")
    print(valid_gauges[:10].tolist())
    print("\nExample removed gauge IDs:")
    removed = percent_error.index.difference(valid_gauges)
    print(removed[:10].tolist())
    print("=================================")
cls

import rasterio`
from shapely.geometry import Point`
`
dem = rasterio.open(r"C:\Users\hdagne1\Downloads\USGS_1M_16_x38y388_AL_17County_2020_B20.tif")`
`
def sample_elevation(point, raster):`
    row, col = raster.index(point.x, point.y)`
    return raster.read(1)[row, col]`
`
def reach_slope(line):`
    start = Point(line.coords[0])`
    end   = Point(line.coords[-1])`
`
    z_start = sample_elevation(start, dem)`
    z_end   = sample_elevation(end, dem)`
`
    length = line.length  # meters`
    slope = (z_start - z_end) / length if length > 0 else None`
`
    return z_start, z_end, slope`
`
gdf[["z_start", "z_end", "slope"]] = gdf.geometry.apply(`
    lambda g: reach_slope(g)`
).apply(pd.Series)ccc
